# Legion Foundation Stage 3 — independent pass A


Scope: blind comparison of 109 committed Legion atoms against current Legion plus 18 frozen donors. Requested=109×19; evaluated=2,071 mechanism slots; unresolved=all `Unclear` entries; excluded=lane B, prior Foundation work, pending material, preservation maps, forbidden documents, web, agent artifacts & generated outputs. No tests, builds, generators, installs or delivery operations ran.

Baseline identity: legion_current=ad3d27c6d3d0d04fd7cb850416baa9793db6c9c4+worktree:2200f46733c67328e3158cd0062d8a376eb39dba; addyosmani__agent-skills=d2c37ef6225dd8726cdd369a8030307f48592d26; Agent-Field__agentfield=8bc5e808b1481554628f14fdc98c72e3ad89fb32; Agent-Field__SWE-AF=0c64fe7cc4fc216f4d32d0b855015509750eb4aa; anthropics__claude-code=f1af9b1f4b1fd4c776135381606edada82ef638e; ArabelaTso__Coding-Skills-Collection=e66a625f0dd4e3ce2cdcf7ddcc81eec89b237bf4; coderabbitai__skills=aa49953c4cb2590e35480637b1b6a29cf4187cfa; EricGrill__agents-skills-plugins=43a037fd7952cc5205bafe146f33369e518e528b; garrytan__gstack=07b59e396c6be5a86619a43151cb9ed62a15ae69; instructa__agent-skills=7e90526cebf7fa6d15bab3260061d1ca383225b3; LambdaTest__agent-skills=0491a3a29aa18558d2c3c64ff09367adb976c56f; mattpocock__skills=6654f6b60cd9d5be8b54c6fafe44346dabeb3b76; NeoLabHQ__context-engineering-kit=23e2428e809d77717f8acc9659c374a3a1fcb93e; obra__superpowers=b36e0829c6d0140e93cfef2ca599b1b07d4a7797; SWE-agent__mini-swe-agent=25941c89cfbc91eb40b3f8756348c91d9977d57e; swe-agent__swe-agent=3ea751c087f32b16e039a2233dd6eefecef325d5; testdino-hq__playwright-skill=d3be9ca4d7303e2aee3eba4842963abf573117b0; trailofbits__skills=d1f1575cff97816e5cc08af66cd2506099c681d3; VoltAgent__awesome-agent-skills=5e1f3aebcf5de90b5b11fb35a607f95bdddf987e. Current dirty baseline is limited to `skills/dispatch/SKILL.md` per frozen corpus manifest; this pass neither read nor modified that file.

Evidence rule: `Observed` requires target, production file/symbol, live consumer, mechanism, state/persistence/fallback & safeguard/test. `Unclear` names partial mechanism plus exact gap. `Not found` means scoped operative-source search found no satisfying mechanism. Generated output never proves source behavior.

Mechanism totals: Observed=98; Unclear=93; Not found=1880; N/A=0; total=2,071.
